1000_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:1000
			exists = character:1001
		}
		character:1000 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:1001 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

		limit = {
			exists = character:83053
			exists = character:83050
		}
		character:83053 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:83050 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

		limit = {
			exists = character:83594
			exists = character:7197
		}
		character:83594 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:7197 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

		limit = {
			exists = character:166026
			exists = character:166021
		}
		character:166026 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:166021 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

		limit = {
			exists = character:166106
			exists = character:131505
		}
		character:166106 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:131505 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
